- **conventions**: - A parameter noun before a measurement (`при температуре 660 °C`, `со скоростью 60 км/ч`, `диаметром 5 м`) is named by that measurement, so the measurement is an apposition and stays nominative in any case the parameter itself carries. The genitive reading needs an explicit `в` in the source: `при температуре в 660 градусов`. The closed list of these nouns lives in `MEASURE_PARAMETER_HEADS`. - A ([NickZaitsev/ru-normalizr](https://github.com/NickZaitsev/ru-normalizr/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/conventions.md) · ★30)

- **cook-verify**: jeff `verify` stage for operation tasks. Independently verify every deterministic postcondition and acceptance criterion after execution. Verdict pass or needs-work; do not edit state. ([johanthoren/jeff](https://github.com/johanthoren/jeff/blob/HEAD/agents/cook-verify.md) · ★6)

Trigger on "add route X", "implement handler Y", "service for Z", "wire up middleware W", or any change to backend/internal/{handler,service,server}. Enforces strict handler→service→store layering — no HTTP types in services, no business logic in handlers. Runs go vet + race-tests before declaring done.
